- **daily-restart-wave**: Pre-emptive mass-restart of every team agent once per 24h for context freshness. Owned by the Dottore. Runs only inside a narrow daily window (default 03:00 UTC ± 30 min) and only if no wave has fired in the last 23h. Each agent is killed + respawned via the same atomic sequence of `liveness-check` Step 3, ordered tier 3 → tier 2 → tier 1 so the workers cycle first and the coordinators (Capitano/Sentinella/Mentor/Assistente) last. Background: Codex/Kimi long-lived sessions accumulate "noise" — old decisions, stale facts, prompt drift — and become measurably less lucid after hours.
